   - **Standing constraints (still in force):**
     - Commit only when asked (nothing is committed).
     - "Published" means a paper; do NOT post, submit, send upstream or open PRs without asking.
     - Do not edit `agent_cache/scripts/`, launchers (`c26.sh`, `watch.sh`) or frozen engines while a campaign runs. Own analysis scripts in `frozen/oracle_scripts_staging/c26_ec/` and `results/campaign26/checks/` are fine (the launcher never calls them).
     - Stop runs only for errors; a slowdown is a result.
     - Queue changes are the user's call ("Keep the queue as it is").
     - Host is preemptible: on a shutdown, empty `results/.current_run` first (`: > results/.current_run`), `docker start sglang_hicache`, relaunch at once: `docker exec -d sglang_hicache bash -c 'nohup bash /sgl-workspace/sglang/agent_cache/frozen/oracle_scripts_staging/c26_ec/c26.sh >> /sgl-workspace/sglang/agent_cache/results/campaign26/launcher.log 2>&1'`. Finished arms are skipped; an interrupted arm reruns from scratch.
     - Host /tmp (incl. scratchpad) is wiped on reboot; keep durable work in `agent_cache/frozen/` or `results/`.
     - Report times to the user in ET (EST = UTC-5); records such as DECISIONS.md keep UTC ("Z") (my interpretation; user did not object).
     - Doc style: concise, plain, no [RH]-style tags; repo rules: ASCII comments of 1-2 lines, msgspec not dataclasses, no defensive getattr.
     - Privacy (harness): never put the user's name/email/project in requests to outside services.
     - Multi-agent workflows: not opted into; do not start unless asked.
     - A built-in safety check refused `rm -f results/_trial_c128/d/*` (glob after `cd`). Do not work around it or retry that removal; removal of the trial folder `agent_cache/results/_trial_c128/` (now 111 MB) is the user's call (already told the user it is left over and safe to remove).
     - The harness blocks foreground `sleep`; wait for background-task notifications instead; do not chain sleeps.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **Setting:** 44 users, 70 GB host pool, gaps x10, arrival 0.1 users/s, 768 users, 50 turns, seed 0, 2 h per arm; hints `dist` (p10/p50/p90 + end-of-session) or `oracle` (exact). GPU pool 668,160 tokens; host pool 712,128 tokens at 70 GB (room beyond the write-through mirror 44K tokens). Second setting: 128 users, 160 GB. GPU unit since 15:31Z 12 Nov: `GPU-6374d407`.
   - **Engine flags:** `--hicache-storage-next-turn-read-timing {just_in_time,expected_cost,arrival}`, `--hicache-storage-next-turn-memory-price` (5e-5), `--hicache-storage-next-turn-price-learning {off,on,harm}`, `--hicache-storage-next-turn-soft-pins {off,on}`, `--hicache-storage-next-turn-landing-quantile` (fq90: 0.9), `--radix-eviction-policy return_chance` with `{"horizon_s":30}` / `{"score":"lru"}` / `{"score":"queue"}`; arrival gate `on` in all `owned` arms. Engines: `engine_ec_20261111d` (all rules), `engine_ec_20261111e` (d + score `queue`), `engine_owned_20261101`, `engine_5df71c4bf0_v2`.
   - **Rule 2 in the engine (`soft_pin_victims` in `frozen/engine_ec_20261111d/python/sglang/srt/mem_cache/next_turn_prefetch.py`, lines 1207-1252):** when the read of a queued (arrived) turn needs room, staged reads of turns not yet arrived are given up, least likely back within their own read time first (`chance = plan.dist.chance_within(idle_s, horizon_s=read seconds)`); each bump calls `self._price.refuse(tokens)` and `self._price.charge(chance * read_seconds)` and logs `HICACHE_EVT ec_bump rid=<bumped turn> for_rid=<arrived turn> tokens=N chance=C`. `_release_staged` logs `ec_release` (pin given back when the entry has passed). Rule 4 `_follow_room` logs `ec_price price refused idle excess room held staged harm_ms`.
   - **Rule 1 decision log (`ec_start`):** `decision=arrival` (left to arrival), `now` (started before arrival), `wait` (timed for later); one line per change of decision. A bump resets `plan.decision = ""`.
   - **Arm labels (44 users):** `baseline`/`base_rep`, `hintfree`/`hintfree_ctl`, `jit_p50`/`jit_p50_ctl`/`jit_p50_d`, `jit_exact`/`jit_exact_d`, `oracle_v2`, `ec_r1`, `ec_r13`/`ec_r13_rep`, `ec_r134`, `ec_full` (DONE), `fq90` (RUNNING), `ec_r3` (queued), `nofc`/`nofc_rep`, `nofc_noend`/`nofc_noend_rep`. Runs: `ec_full` = `20261112_214834_three_tier_wc_oracle_v2_nu_owned_custom_L1nat_H70` (client `client_215131_cmp_three_tier_wc_oracle_v2_nu_owned`); `ec_r134` = `20261112_153600_..._H70`; `ec_r13` = `20261111_070012_..._H70`; `ec_r13_rep` = `20261111_224624_..._H70`; `ec_r1` = `20261111_110558_..._H70`; `fq90` = `20261112_235315_three_tier_wc_oracle_v2_nu_owned_custom_L1nat_H70` (client `client_235446_...`). 128-user labels as before (`ec_r134_c128` = `20261112_194449_three_tier_wc_oracle_v2_nu_owned_NAT160`, etc.).
   - **Rule for reading repeats (pre-registered):** a difference between two arms counts as shown when every pair of their runs has the same sign and the smallest difference exceeds the largest move between two runs of either arm. `ec_full`, `ec_r134`, `ec_r1` have one run each.
   - **Figure conventions:** colours baseline #2a78d6, old oracle #4a3aa7, JIT p50 #a0307a, JIT exact #1baf7a, pricing arms #eb6834 (rules 1+3 solid, rules 1+3+4 dash-dot `(0, (5, 1.5, 1, 1.5))`, all four rules dotted `(0, (1, 1.5))`); deck style = `scripts/jit_vs_hicache.py` (5-min bins, top legend, labelled line ends, footnote with numbers, PNG+PDF+CSV).
   - **Tools:** host python stdlib only (`nice -n 10 python3 -I -B`, from `agent_cache/`); matplotlib and PIL in the container: `docker exec sglang_hicache bash -c 'cd /sgl-workspace/sglang/agent_cache/scripts && CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=7 PYTHONPATH=. nice -n 15 python3 -B ../frozen/oracle_scripts_staging/c26_ec/<script>.py'`. Shell is zsh (bare `====` in echo fails; unquoted `--include=*.py` globs fail with "no matches found"); `cd` carries between calls, so start every command with `cd /home/wanhr/sglang/agent_cache`. `TZ=America/New_York date` for ET. Container sees the repo at `/sgl-workspace/sglang` but NOT the host /tmp scratchpad (trial outputs go under `results/_trial_c128/d/`).

     ```python
     import matplotlib.pyplot as plt  # noqa: E402
     from matplotlib.lines import Line2D  # noqa: E402
     from matplotlib.ticker import FuncFormatter, NullLocator  # noqa: E402
     ...
     NCOL = 3  # legend columns at most; fewer when the labels would run past the figure's right edge
     LEGEND = dict(frameon=False, fontsize=9.5, handlelength=1.8, columnspacing=1.6)
     WIDTH_IN, LEFT, DPI = 8.6, 0.085, 180  # figure width; where the title, legend, panels and footnote start, as a share of it; PNG dpi
     WRAP = 132  # footnote characters per line at this width and 8 pt
     ...
     def legend_columns(labels):
         """The most columns, up to NCOL, at which the legend ends inside the figure; measured on a scratch figure."""
         for ncol in range(NCOL, 1, -1):
             # Same width, anchor and dpi as the saved figure, so the box ends where the real legend will.
             fig = plt.figure(figsize=(WIDTH_IN, 1), dpi=DPI)
             box = fig.legend([Line2D([], [])] * len(labels), row_major(labels, ncol), loc="upper left", bbox_to_anchor=(LEFT, 1), ncol=ncol, **LEGEND)
             fig.canvas.draw()
             fits = box.get_window_extent().x1 <= fig.bbox.x1
             plt.close(fig)
             if fits:
                 return ncol
         return 1
     ...
         ncol = legend_columns([a.label for a in arms])
         legend_rows = -(-len(arms) // ncol)
         top_in, bottom_in = 0.85 + 0.22 * legend_rows, 0.62 + 0.165 * len(lines)
         height = PANEL_IN * len(args.panels) + top_in + bottom_in
         fig, axes = plt.subplots(len(args.panels), 1, figsize=(WIDTH_IN, height), sharex=True, facecolor=jv.SURFACE, squeeze=False)
     ...
         fig.text(LEFT, 1 - 0.14 / height, args.title or figset["title"], fontsize=12, color=jv.INK, va="top", weight="semibold")
         fig.legend(row_major(handles, ncol), row_major(labels, ncol), loc="upper left", bbox_to_anchor=(LEFT, 1 - 0.42 / height), ncol=ncol, labelcolor=jv.INK,
                    **LEGEND)
         jv.footnote(fig, LEFT, "\n".join(lines))
         fig.subplots_adjust(top=1 - top_in / height, bottom=bottom_in / height, left=LEFT, right=0.86, hspace=0.42)
     ...
             fig.savefig(os.path.join(out, f"{args.name}.{ext}"), dpi=DPI, facecolor=jv.SURFACE)
     ```

   - **`agent_cache/results/campaign26/checks/bumps.py`** (NEW kept check) and **`checks/bumps.out`**:
     ```python
     """Rule 2 (soft pins): what the bumped staged reads cost, and what the turns they made room for got.
     From agent_cache/:  nice -n 10 python3 -I -B results/campaign26/checks/bumps.py label=run [reference=run]
     An ec_bump line is a staged read given up so that the read of a turn that has arrived finds room. For the first run:
     the bumps and the SSD pages their reads had loaded, when the bumped turns arrived and whether they read again, and the
     SSD pages by what became of the read. With a reference run (the same arm without soft pins): TTFT of the bumped
     turns, of the turns the room went to and of the other returning turns, beside the same turns there.
     Times are seconds; a turn is a (user, turn) pair, and only turns both runs recorded are set side by side."""
     import collections, datetime as dt, json, os, re, statistics as st, sys

     EVT = re.compile(r"^\[(.{23})\] HICACHE_EVT (ec_bump|s2h_io|ntp_arrive|ntp_stage) rid=\S+?-c(\d+)-t(\d+)(.*)")
     FOR = re.compile(r"for_rid=\S+?-c(\d+)-t(\d+) tokens=(\d+) chance=([\d.]+)")
     PAGES = re.compile(r"pages=(\d+)")
     stamp = lambda s: dt.datetime.strptime(s, "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S.%f").replace(tzinfo=dt.timezone.utc).timestamp()
     q = lambda v, p: sorted(v)[min(len(v) - 1, int(p * len(v)))] if v else float("nan")
     three = lambda v: f"{q(v, .1):.1f} {q(v, .5):.1f} {q(v, .9):.1f}"

     def turns(run):
         """(user, turn) -> the client's record, for the turns that got a first token."""
         client = sorted(d for d in os.listdir(f"results/{run}") if d.startswith("client_"))[-1]
         rows = (json.loads(line) for line in open(f"results/{run}/{client}/client.jsonl") if line.strip())
         return {(r["conv"], r["turn"]): r for r in rows if r.get("kind") == "turn" and r.get("ttft") is not None}

     def beside(name, keys, own, other):
         """Mean TTFT of the keys in the run, and against the reference on the keys both have."""
         ... (prints count, mean and median TTFT; on keys in both runs: means, paired mean, faster/slower by over 0.05 s)

     (name, run), ref = sys.argv[1].split("="), (sys.argv[2].split("=") if len(sys.argv) > 2 else None)
     ... (parses ec_bump, s2h_io, ntp_arrive, ntp_stage; prints: bumps of staged reads, turns, arrived turns served, tokens, chance p10/p50/p90;
          pages the bumped reads had loaded and how long the data sat; bump-to-arrival times and what followed
          (read again before arrival / read at arrival / no further read / never arrived); SSD pages by read fate
          (at or after arrival / staged and kept / staged, then bumped); then beside() for bumped turns, turns the room went to, other returning turns)
     ```

SSD pages their reads had loaded 80,955 (13.7 % of the run's 591,280 whole-log pages); data had sat 0.3/2.4/13.9 s. Bump to arrival 1.3/8.4/63.8 s; within 1 s 39, within 5 s 163 of 462; read at arrival 260, read again before arrival 192, no further read 10. Pages by read: at or after arrival 420,165 (71 %), staged and kept 90,160 (15 %), staged then bumped 80,955 (14 %).    - **Landing quantile in the engine (`frozen/engine_ec_20261111d/python/sglang/srt/mem_cache/next_turn_prefetch.py`):** in `_resolve_hint`, `early_s = entry.p50 - dist.quantile(self._landing_quantile)` when the flag is set (negative for 0.9: the read lands at the gap's p90), else `early_s = max(0.0, entry.p50 - entry.p10)` (the base JIT p50 arm lands at the p10). Negative `early_s` is handled on purpose: plan `expires_at = predicted + max(0.0, -hint.early_s) + max(MIN_EXPIRY_S, hint.next_gap_s)`; `read_deadline` returns `max(deadline - min(0.0, plan.early_s), now + NTP_JIT_MARGIN_S)` ("Started on purpose past the p50, so it stays a timed read"); `trigger_time(admission_at, load_s, margin_s=plan.early_s + NTP_JIT_MARGIN_S)`. The flag requires `just_in_time` timing (validated in `hicache_hook.py`).
   - **Unpin reason `abort`:** in JIT arms it is the plan timing out (`_expire`: "Drop a plan whose turn never arrived", logs `ntp_expire ... reason=timeout`; fq90 had 98 with `staged=1`, 38 with `staged=0`; `jit_p50_d` 103 / 76); in pricing arms it is rule 1's release (`ec_release`; `ec_r1` has no `ntp_expire` lines) or a rule-2 bump (`ec_bump`).
   - **Pin events:** `srs_pin rid=... tokens=N node=... pinned=LEVEL` is logged when a read lands; `srs_unpin rid=... tokens=N reason={admit,abort,replaced} pinned=LEVEL`; `ntp_arrive rid=...` marks the turn's arrival; `ntp_stage` a read staged before arrival; `srs_dispatch ... launched={0,1}` (0 = refused room); compare_ec's "mean pinned tokens" is the time-weighted mean of the `pinned=` level over the kept part of the run.

   - **`agent_cache/results/campaign26/checks/pin_hold.py`** (NEW kept check; final version) and **`checks/pin_hold.out`**:
     ```python
     """Host memory held by read pins, split by whether the read landed before its turn arrived.
     From agent_cache/:  nice -n 10 python3 -I -B results/campaign26/checks/pin_hold.py label=run [label=run ...]
     Per run, from server.log's srs_pin, srs_unpin and ntp_arrive lines: every pin is paired with the next unpin of the same
     turn and counted as tokens x seconds held. An early pin is one held over EARLY s before its turn arrived (to its unpin
     when the turn never came); its hold is split at the arrival into the wait for the turn and the wait in the queue. The
     mean level is all token-seconds over the span from the first to the last pin event, and each part's share of it is
     printed in tokens. Times are seconds; quantiles are p10 p50 p90."""
     import collections, datetime as dt, re, sys

     EVT = re.compile(r"^\[(.{23})\] HICACHE_EVT (srs_pin|srs_unpin|ntp_arrive) rid=\S+?-c(\d+)-t(\d+) (.*)")
     TOKENS = re.compile(r"tokens=(\d+)")
     REASON = re.compile(r"reason=(\w+)")
     stamp = lambda s: dt.datetime.strptime(s, "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S.%f").replace(tzinfo=dt.timezone.utc).timestamp()
     q = lambda v, p: sorted(v)[min(len(v) - 1, int(p * len(v)))] if v else float("nan")
     three = lambda v: f"{q(v, .1):.1f} {q(v, .5):.1f} {q(v, .9):.1f}"
     EARLY = 0.05  # s; a read at arrival logs its pin within a few ms of the arrival line, on either side

     for name, run in (a.split("=") for a in sys.argv[1:]):
         arrive, open_pins, holds, first, last = {}, {}, [], None, None
         for line in open(f"results/{run}/server.log", errors="replace"):
             m = EVT.match(line)
             if not m:
                 continue
             t, kind, key = stamp(m.group(1)), m.group(2), (int(m.group(3)), int(m.group(4)))
             if kind == "ntp_arrive":
                 arrive[key] = t
                 continue
             first, last = first or t, t
             if kind == "srs_pin":
                 open_pins[key] = (t, int(TOKENS.search(m.group(5)).group(1)))
             elif key in open_pins:
                 at, tokens = open_pins.pop(key)
                 holds.append((key, at, t, tokens, REASON.search(m.group(5)).group(1)))
         span = last - first
         # Seconds a pin was held before its turn arrived; negative when it was taken after the arrival.
         wait = lambda h: min(h[2], arrive.get(h[0], h[2])) - h[1]
         early = [h for h in holds if wait(h) > EARLY]
         late = [h for h in holds if wait(h) <= EARLY]
         area = lambda hs: sum(tokens * (end - at) for _, at, end, tokens, _ in hs)
         before = sum(h[3] * wait(h) for h in early)
         waits = [wait(h) for h in early]
         reasons = collections.Counter(h[4] for h in early)
         print(f"{name} {run}")
         print(f"  pins {len(holds)} paired ({len(open_pins)} still held at the end), mean level {area(holds) / span:,.0f} tokens over {span / 60:.0f} min")
         print(f"  early pins {len(early)} on {len({h[0] for h in early})} turns: {three([h[3] for h in early])} tokens; held before the arrival {three(waits)} s, "
               f"over 30 s {sum(w > 30 for w in waits)}, over 120 s {sum(w > 120 for w in waits)}; ended by " + ", ".join(f"{k} {n}" for k, n in reasons.most_common()))
         print(f"    token-seconds: before the arrival {before / 1e6:,.1f} M ({before / span:,.0f} tokens of the mean level), after it {(area(early) - before) / 1e6:,.1f} M ({(area(early) - before) / span:,.0f})")
         print(f"  the other pins {len(late)}: held {three([h[2] - h[1] for h in late])} s; token-seconds {area(late) / 1e6:,.1f} M ({area(late) / span:,.0f} tokens of the mean level)")
     ```
     Output (seven runs): `jit_p50_d` 3,286 pins, mean level 32,398; early pins 1,182 (1,174 turns), held before arrival 1.1/5.3/41.0 s, 160 over 30 s, ended admit 783 / replaced 335 / abort 64; 164.0 M token-seconds before arrival = 24,573 tokens of the mean level. `fq90` 2,239 pins, mean 16,342; early 419 (410 turns), tokens 1,472/14,656/29,760, held 0.5/3.0/60.2 s, 75 over 30 s, 0 over 120 s, ended admit 270 / abort 82 / replaced 67; 87.6 M = 13,181 tokens; other pins 1,820 (2,586 tokens). `ec_r1` 2,014 pins, mean 5,457; early 367, held 0.5/2.2/12.0 s, 13 over 30 s, 3 over 120 s, admit 272 / replaced 63 / abort 32; 17.8 M = 2,676 tokens. `ec_r13` 327 early, 0.7/2.9/13.3 s, 18 over 30 s, 15.6 M = 2,360. `ec_r13_rep` 293 early, 0.5/3.7/16.4 s, 20 over 30 s, 13.4 M = 2,035. `nofc` 2 early (0.1-0.5 s, log-order artifacts), 0 tokens; mean level 2,910. `jit_exact_d` 1,724 pins, mean 7,130; early 864, held 0.7/1.7/2.4 s, none over 30 s; 26.4 M = 4,041 tokens.

This is the deck's argument for rule 1 seen in a run, with rule 1's edge over the better fixed quantile tried at 0.09-0.27 s on one run each. It changes nothing against the rivals that start no read early (they hold no room before arrival and stand where rule 1 does).

   - **Standing constraints (still in force):**
     - Commit only when asked (nothing is committed).
     - "Published" means a paper; do NOT post, submit, send upstream or open PRs without asking.
     - Do not edit `agent_cache/scripts/`, launchers (`c26.sh`, `watch.sh`) or frozen engines while a campaign runs. Own analysis scripts in `frozen/oracle_scripts_staging/c26_ec/` and `results/campaign26/checks/` are fine (the launcher never calls them).
     - Stop runs only for errors; a slowdown is a result.
     - Queue changes are the user's call ("Keep the queue as it is").
     - Host is preemptible: on a shutdown, empty `results/.current_run` first (`: > results/.current_run`), `docker start sglang_hicache`, relaunch at once: `docker exec -d sglang_hicache bash -c 'nohup bash /sgl-workspace/sglang/agent_cache/frozen/oracle_scripts_staging/c26_ec/c26.sh >> /sgl-workspace/sglang/agent_cache/results/campaign26/launcher.log 2>&1'`. Finished arms are skipped; an interrupted arm reruns from scratch. Then start the watcher (now `bash results/campaign26/checks/watch_all.sh`, see below).
     - Host /tmp (incl. scratchpad) is wiped on reboot; keep durable work in `agent_cache/frozen/` or `results/`.
     - Report times to the user in ET (EST = UTC-5); records such as DECISIONS.md keep UTC ("Z") (my interpretation; user did not object).
     - Doc style: concise, plain, no [RH]-style tags; repo rules: ASCII comments of 1-2 lines, msgspec not dataclasses, no defensive getattr.
     - Privacy (harness): never put the user's name/email/project in requests to outside services.
     - Multi-agent workflows: not opted into; do not start unless asked.
     - A built-in safety check refused `rm -f results/_trial_c128/d/*` (glob after `cd`). Do not work around it or retry that removal; removal of the trial folder `agent_cache/results/_trial_c128/` (111 MB) is the user's call (already told the user it is left over and safe to remove).
     - The harness blocks foreground `sleep`; wait for background-task notifications instead; do not chain sleeps.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **Setting:** 44 users, 70 GB host pool, gaps x10, arrival 0.1 users/s, 768 users, 50 turns, seed 0, 2 h per arm; hints `dist` (p10/p50/p90 + end-of-session) or `oracle` (exact). GPU pool 668,160 tokens; host pool 712,128 tokens at 70 GB (44K tokens of room beyond the write-through mirror), 1,017,280 tokens at 100 GB (about 349K tokens of room), 1,627,648 tokens at 160 GB. Second setting: 128 users, 160 GB (run folders end `_NAT160`). GPU unit since 15:31Z 12 Nov: `GPU-6374d407`.
   - **Engine flags:** `--hicache-storage-next-turn-read-timing {just_in_time,expected_cost,arrival}`, `--hicache-storage-next-turn-memory-price` (5e-5), `--hicache-storage-next-turn-price-learning {off,on,harm}`, `--hicache-storage-next-turn-soft-pins {off,on}`, `--hicache-storage-next-turn-landing-quantile` (fq90: 0.9), `--radix-eviction-policy return_chance` with `{"horizon_s":30}` / `{"score":"lru"}` / `{"score":"queue"}`; default eviction `next_use` with `{'horizon_s': 300}`; arrival gate `on` in all `owned` arms. Engines: `engine_ec_20261111d` (all rules), `engine_ec_20261111e` (d + score `queue`), `engine_owned_20261101`, `engine_5df71c4bf0_v2`.
   - **Arm labels (44 users, 70 GB):** `baseline`/`base_rep`, `hintfree`/`hintfree_ctl`, `jit_p50`/`jit_p50_ctl`/`jit_p50_d`, `jit_exact`/`jit_exact_d`, `oracle_v2`, `ec_r1`, `ec_r13`/`ec_r13_rep`, `ec_r134`, `ec_full`, `fq90`, `ec_r3` (DONE this window), `nofc`/`nofc_rep`, `nofc_noend`/`nofc_noend_rep`. 128 users: `baseline`, `oracle`, `p50` (campaigns 19/20), `jit_exact_c128`, `jit_p50_c128`, `hintfree_c128`, `nofc_noend_c128`, `ec_r134_c128`, `nofc_c128` (DONE this window). 100 GB at 44 users: `nofc_noend_h100` (RUNNING), `jit_p50_h100`, `jit_exact_h100`.
   - **Runs:** `ec_r3` = `20261113_015606_three_tier_wc_oracle_v2_nu_owned_custom_L1nat_H70` (client `client_015737_cmp_three_tier_wc_oracle_v2_nu_owned`); `nofc_c128` = `20261113_035820_three_tier_wc_oracle_v2_nu_owned_NAT160` (client `client_040021_...`); `nofc_noend_h100` = `20261113_060157_three_tier_wc_oracle_v2_nu_owned_custom_L1nat_H100` (client `client_060658_...`); `fq90` = `20261112_235315_..._H70`; `ec_full` = `20261112_214834_..._H70`; `ec_r134` = `20261112_153600_..._H70`; `ec_r13` = `20261111_070012_..._H70`; `ec_r13_rep` = `20261111_224624_..._H70`; `ec_r1` = `20261111_110558_..._H70`; `nofc` = `20261111_090311_..._H70`; `jit_p50_d` = `20261111_204059_..._H70`; `jit_exact_d` = `20261112_045511_..._H70`; 128-user order run `20261018_154254_three_tier_wc_oracle_v2_nu_NAT160`. Manifest: `results/campaign26/manifest.txt` (a line is added when an arm ends); runs outside it in `extra_runs.txt` (`oracle_v2`, `jit_p50_d_cut`, `baseline_c128`, `oracle_v2_c128`, `jit_p50_c128`, `jit_exact_c128`).
   - **Loader rule for other settings:** in `compare_ec.py`, `if (f[0].endswith("_c128") or re.search(r"_h\d+$", f[0])) and f[0] not in sys.argv[1:]:` skips the arm, so `_c128` and `_h100` manifest arms join `compare_ec.py` / `pairs_ec.py` / `stall_ec.py` / `checks/latency.py` only when named; when arguments are given only the named arms are kept. The 70 GB tables therefore stay at 20 runs.
   - **Rule for reading repeats (pre-registered):** a difference between two arms counts as shown when every pair of their runs has the same sign and the smallest difference exceeds the largest move between two runs of either arm. `ec_r3`, `fq90`, `ec_full`, `ec_r134`, `ec_r1` have one run each; no arm has a repeat at 128 users.
   - **Figure conventions:** colours baseline #2a78d6, old oracle #4a3aa7, JIT p50 #a0307a, JIT exact #1baf7a, pricing arms #eb6834; deck style = `scripts/jit_vs_hicache.py`. `figs_designs.sh` rule loop: `for arm in ec_r1 ec_r3 ec_r13 ec_r13_rep ec_r134 ec_full` (timelines + wait split); `compare.png` / `pricing_vs_jit.png` compare seven arms (HiCache, old oracle, JIT p50, JIT exact, rules 1+3, rules 1+3+4, all four rules). `figs_designs_c128.sh` covers `baseline_c128 oracle_v2_c128 jit_p50_c128 jit_exact_c128` + `ec_r134_c128 ec_full_c128` (rivals are not in either designs set).
   - **Tools:** host python stdlib only (`nice -n 10 python3 -I -B`, from `agent_cache/`); matplotlib and PIL in the container: `docker exec sglang_hicache bash /sgl-workspace/sglang/agent_cache/frozen/oracle_scripts_staging/c26_ec/figs_designs.sh` (about 100 s). Shell is zsh (quote globs); start every command with `cd /home/wanhr/sglang/agent_cache` (a `cd` to the memory folder resets the shell cwd to `/home/wanhr/sglang`). `TZ=America/New_York date` for ET. Table commands: `compare_ec.py` (~48 s), `pairs_ec.py [arms...]` (~10-55 s), `stall_ec.py [arms...]` (env `STALL_LIMIT_S=6900 STALL_ORDER_RUN=<run> STALL_CSV=<path>` for 128 users), `compare_c128.py` (~38 s), `results/campaign26/checks/latency.py arm [arm ...]`, `checks/boot.py [run folder]`, `checks/bumps.py`, `checks/pin_hold.py label=run ...`. TaskStop is a deferred tool (load with `ToolSearch select:TaskStop`).
   - **Timing pattern:** an arm takes about 2 h 02-04 min from launch to DONE; after a 160 GB arm the next server loads weights from a cold page cache (212 s this time instead of ~13 s); the client starts only once the server is ready, so the run is unaffected.

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - **`agent_cache/results/campaign26/checks/watch_all.sh`** (NEW this window; the watcher to use from now on). Created because `c26_ec/watch.sh` runs its problem checks only for run folders matching `*_NAT160|*custom_L1nat_H70*`, which skips the `_H100` runs. `watch.sh` itself is left unedited. Full content:
     ```bash
     #!/usr/bin/env bash
     # c26_ec/watch.sh with its problem checks widened to every host pool size (that one skips run folders ending in
     # _H100, so the three 100 GB arms ran unchecked). From agent_cache/, on the host:  bash results/campaign26/checks/watch_all.sh
     # Exits on the next arm's end in campaign 26's launcher.log or on a problem: a traceback in the running server's log,
     # client errors, a client that stops writing for 300 s, or the container down.
     L=results/campaign26/launcher.log
     P='C26 .*(DONE|STOP|IDLE)'
     n0=$(grep -cE "$P" "$L")
     while :; do
       n=$(grep -cE "$P" "$L")
       if [ "$n" -gt "$n0" ]; then echo "launcher: $(grep -E "$P" "$L" | tail -1 | cut -c1-240)"; exit 0; fi
       R=$(cat results/.current_run 2>/dev/null)
       case "$R" in *_NAT160|*custom_L1nat_H*)
         if [ -f "results/$R/server.log" ]; then
           tb=$(grep -ac 'Traceback' "results/$R/server.log")
           [ "$tb" -gt 0 ] && { echo "ALERT: $tb tracebacks in results/$R/server.log"; exit 0; }
         fi
         for c in results/$R/client_*/client.jsonl; do
           [ -f "$c" ] || continue
           e=$(grep -ac '"error": "' "$c")
           [ "$e" -gt 0 ] && { echo "ALERT: $e client errors in $c"; exit 0; }
           age=$(( $(date +%s) - $(stat -c %Y "$c") ))
           [ "$age" -gt 300 ] && { echo "ALERT: $c has not grown for $age s"; exit 0; }
         done ;;
       esac
       docker ps --format '{{.Names}}' | grep -q '^sglang_hicache$' || { echo "ALERT: container down"; exit 0; }
       sleep 60
     done
     ```

4. Errors and fixes:
   - **Stale system note that watcher `bqw4op3fg` was "still running":** the task notification and its output showed "[exited with code 0]" and no watcher process existed, so I launched a new one without TaskStop (same situation as in earlier windows).
   - **Regression-check parser for the pair table:** the first version did not recognise the column-header line (arm names contain digits), so it reported "old cells 0" and treated matrix rows as plain rows; fixed by detecting the header as a line whose tokens are all arm names. Result: 513 old cells unchanged. The one remaining "changed row" was an artefact of keying the between-arms rows by their first word.
   - **`pin_hold.out` install did not run:** the check `diff <(grep -v -A4 '^ec_r3 ' ...)` did not remove the `ec_r3` block, so `diff` returned non-zero and the chained `cp` was skipped; redone with `diff <(sed '6,10d' new) old` (identical), then copied.
   - **Wrong phrase in the `nofc_c128` record entry:** "the six arms of this build family that protect queued turns" (the old oracle is an older build) → corrected to "six of the seven arms that protect queued turns".
   - **Watcher gap found:** `c26_ec/watch.sh` checks tracebacks / client errors / stalls only for run folders matching `*_NAT160|*custom_L1nat_H70*`; the `_h100` arms (appended to the queue after the watcher was written) would have been watched for their end only. Fix: new `results/campaign26/checks/watch_all.sh` with the pattern `*_NAT160|*custom_L1nat_H*`; stopped the old watcher task `bssr87hsf` with TaskStop and started `watch_all.sh` as task `btju5omln` at about 06:06Z, before the first 100 GB arm's client started. `watch.sh` was not edited (standing caution about editing launcher-side scripts while a campaign runs).
   - **Slow server start of `nofc_noend_h100`** (4.5 min instead of ~1.5): diagnosed from server.log as the weights loading from a cold page cache after the 160 GB arm (`load_weight=211.85` s); benign, the same pattern as after earlier 160 GB arms; the client starts only when the server is ready.
   - Tables are written to the scratchpad first and copied into `results/campaign26/` only after a regression check (old rows unchanged), so an interrupted run cannot clobber a kept table.

5. Problem Solving:
   - **`ec_r3` (44 users, 70 GB; rule 3 alone = JIT p50 timing landing at the p10 with return-chance eviction, 30 s horizon; done 10:58 PM ET Thu 12 Nov, clean, 10,409 turns):** 1.48 s on the 6,715 shared turns against 1.35-1.60 s for the three JIT p50 runs (differences -0.12, +0.13, +0.00 s; on the 9,690 turns the four share -0.04, +0.09, -0.02 s; five of six intervals include zero; same-build control `jit_p50_d` +0.004 / -0.019 s).
